// Auto-discovered symbols that are NOT independently identified/named.
//
// These addresses are referenced by name (jump-table entries, cross-file
// calls) from code in one of the manually-split cpp segments, but splat's
// own create_undefined_funcs_auto / create_undefined_syms_auto mechanism
// can't recover them automatically: the bytes at these addresses get
// silently absorbed into the body of whichever named function precedes
// them in the containing cpp segment, so splat's split-time analysis
// never considers them "undefined" -- the failure only shows up later,
// at link time, when a different object references them by symbol name.
// Keeping them in a separate file (rather than symbol_addrs.txt) keeps
// that file free for symbols with an actually-known identity.
//
// type:func  -- entries that need their own INCLUDE_ASM split so they
//               stay independently linkable global functions.
// type:label -- local branch/jump-table targets inside an existing
//               INCLUDE_ASM'd function that must not default to GNU-as
//               local (.L-prefixed) scope, since they're referenced from
//               a separately-compiled object (usually a rodata jump table).

// --- src/visualfx/boardwakefx.cpp ---
